### 6. `src/curve_heads.rs`

Supply only the unchanged original validation fixtures.

**Locate this existing block:**

```text
use session_rust::{Geometry,Arrowhead};
use std::rc::Rc;
pub fn headed(geometry: &Geometry, head: Arrowhead) -> Option<(Geometry, bool)> {
    let out = match geometry {
        Geometry::Line(line) => {
            let mut next = (**line).clone();
            next.arrowhead = head;
            (Geometry::Line(Rc::new(next)), line.arrowhead != head)
        }
        Geometry::Polyline(polyline) => {
            let mut next = (**polyline).clone();
            next.arrowhead = head;
            (
                Geometry::Polyline(Rc::new(next)),
                polyline.arrowhead != head,
            )
        }
        Geometry::NurbsCurve(curve) => {
            let mut next = (**curve).clone();
            next.arrowhead = head;
            (Geometry::NurbsCurve(Rc::new(next)), curve.arrowhead != head)
        }
        _ => return None,
    };
    Some(out)
}
```

### 1. `src/source_edit.rs`

Resolve original planes and eligible text owners.

Replace the entire file with:

```rust
use crate::scene::{ObjectId, Scene};
use session_rust::{Geometry, Xform};
use std::rc::Rc;
pub fn original(scene: &Scene, id: ObjectId) -> Option<Geometry> {
    if let Some(row) = scene.objects().iter().find(|r| r.id == id) {
        if let Some(original)=&row.original{return Some(original.clone())}
        if let Some(cad) = &row.cad {
            return Some(Geometry::BRep(cad.source.clone()));
        }
        if let Some(s) = &row.surface {
            return Some(Geometry::NurbsSurface(s.source.clone()));
        }
        return row.geometry().map(|g| Geometry::Mesh(g.clone()));
    }
    if let Some(row) = scene.points().iter().find(|r| r.id == id) {
        return Some(Geometry::Point(row.prepared.source.clone()));
    }
    if let Some(row)=scene.clouds().iter().find(|r|r.id==id){return Some(Geometry::PointCloud(row.prepared.source.clone()))}
    crate::gather_draft::Draft::source(scene, id)
}
pub fn same(a: &Geometry, b: &Geometry) -> bool {
    match (a, b) {
        (Geometry::PointCloud(a), Geometry::PointCloud(b)) => Rc::ptr_eq(a,b),
        (Geometry::Plane(a), Geometry::Plane(b)) => Rc::ptr_eq(a,b),
        (Geometry::OBB(a), Geometry::OBB(b)) => Rc::ptr_eq(a, b),
        (Geometry::Line(a), Geometry::Line(b)) => Rc::ptr_eq(a, b),
        (Geometry::Polyline(a), Geometry::Polyline(b)) => Rc::ptr_eq(a, b),
        (Geometry::NurbsCurve(a), Geometry::NurbsCurve(b)) => Rc::ptr_eq(a, b),
        (Geometry::Point(a), Geometry::Point(b)) => Rc::ptr_eq(a, b),
        (Geometry::Mesh(a), Geometry::Mesh(b)) => Rc::ptr_eq(a, b),
        (Geometry::BRep(a), Geometry::BRep(b)) => Rc::ptr_eq(a, b),
        (Geometry::NurbsSurface(a), Geometry::NurbsSurface(b)) => Rc::ptr_eq(a, b),
        _ => false,
    }
}
#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct Owner {
    pub id: ObjectId,
    pub source: Geometry,
    pub model: Xform,
}
impl Owner {
    pub fn capture(scene: &Scene, id: ObjectId) -> Result<Self, &'static str> {
        if scene.pick_id(id.value()) != Some(id) {
            return Err("Pick a visible unlocked original");
        }
        Ok(Self {
            id,
            source: original(scene, id).ok_or("Reload an editable original source")?,
            model: scene.placement(id).ok_or("Source placement is missing")?,
        })
    }
    pub fn valid(&self, scene: &Scene) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
        if scene.pick_id(self.id.value()) != Some(self.id)
            || scene.placement(self.id).is_none_or(|p| p.m != self.model.m)
            || original(scene, self.id).is_none_or(|g| !same(&self.source, &g))
        {
            return Err("Original source changed; start again");
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

pub fn editable(scene:&Scene,id:ObjectId)->Result<(),&'static str>{
    if scene.pick_id(id.value())!=Some(id){return Err("Pick a visible unlocked original")}
    if scene.objects().iter().any(|r|r.id==id&&r.original.is_none()&&r.geometry().is_none()){return Err("Reload an editable original source")}
    Ok(())
}
```

```rust
pub fn geometry(scene:&Scene,id:ObjectId)->Result<Geometry,&'static str> {
    let row=scene.objects().iter().find(|row|row.id==id).ok_or("Source object is missing")?;
    if let Some(cad)=&row.cad{return Ok(Geometry::BRep(Rc::clone(&cad.source)))}
    if let Some(surface)=&row.surface{return Ok(Geometry::NurbsSurface(Rc::clone(&surface.source)))}
```

```rust
        for(i,cut)in self.cuts.iter().enumerate(){let back=cut.owner.model.inverse()?;let local=unit_ray(&ray.origin,&ray.direction,&back);for(part,mesh)in cut.parts.hit_meshes(){if cut.removed.contains(&part){continue}if let Some(hits)=intersection::ray_mesh(&local,mesh,1e-12,false){if let Some(hit)=hits.first(){let distance=hit.transformed(&cut.owner.model).distance(&ray.origin,None);if best.is_none_or(|(near,_)|distance<near){best=Some((distance,(i,part)))}}}}}best.map(|(_,hit)|hit)
    }





}
```

```rust
pub fn group(scene:&mut Scene,ids:&[crate::scene::ObjectId],name:&str)->Result<String,&'static str>{
    let crate::group_plan::Plan{owner,document:doc,name,parent,members}=crate::group_plan::Plan::new(scene,ids,name)?;
    let parent_node=&doc.nodes[parent];let parent_world=parent_node.placement.world.clone();let back=parent_world.inverse().ok_or("The layer is degenerate")?;
    let source=session_rust::proto::TreeNode{guid:uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string(),name:name.clone(),..Default::default()};let group_guid=source.guid.clone();
    let mut group=Branch{node:Node{placement:crate::nested_placement::Node{local:Default::default(),world:parent_world.clone(),parent:parent_world.clone()},portable_guid:group_guid.clone(),source,object:None,label:name.clone(),parent:None,depth:0,end:1,readonly:false},children:Vec::new()};let mut tree=branch(&doc,0);
    for id in members{let mut member=take(&mut tree,id).ok_or("Object tree is gone")?;let world=scene.placement(id).ok_or("Source placement is missing")?;member.node.placement=crate::nested_placement::Node{local:&back*&world,world,parent:parent_world.clone()};group.children.push(member);}
    find(&mut tree,&parent_node.source.guid).ok_or("Shared layer is gone")?.children.push(group);let mut nodes=Vec::new();flatten(tree.clone(),None,0,&mut nodes);if nodes.iter().any(|node|node.depth>crate::hierarchy::MAX_DEPTH){return Err("Tree exceeds its depth limit")}
    replace(scene,owner,tree);Rc::make_mut(&mut scene.groups).insert((doc.key,group_guid));Ok(name)
}
```

```rust
fn copy_branch(scene:&mut Scene,mut tree:Branch,all:bool)->Result<Branch,&'static str> {
    tree.node.source.guid=uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();tree.node.portable_guid=tree.node.source.guid.clone();
    if let Some(id)=tree.node.object {let copy=scene.copy_row(id,all)?;tree.node.object=Some(copy);tree.node.source.name=scene.row_guid(copy).unwrap().to_owned();}
    tree.children=tree.children.into_iter().map(|child|copy_branch(scene,child,all)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;Ok(tree)
}
```

```rust
}

fn copy_branch(scene:&mut Scene,mut tree:Branch,all:bool)->Result<Branch,&'static str> {
    let group_doc=scene.forest.documents.iter().find(|d|scene.groups.contains(&(d.key,tree.node.source.guid.clone()))).map(|d|d.key);
    tree.node.source.guid=uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();tree.node.portable_guid=tree.node.source.guid.clone();
    if let Some(key)=group_doc {Rc::make_mut(&mut scene.groups).insert((key,tree.node.source.guid.clone()));}
    if let Some(id)=tree.node.object {let copy=scene.copy_row(id,all)?;tree.node.object=Some(copy);tree.node.source.name=scene.row_guid(copy).unwrap().to_owned();}
    tree.children=tree.children.into_iter().map(|child|copy_branch(scene,child,all)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;Ok(tree)
}
```

```rust
use session_rust::{proto,Session,Xform};
use prost::Message;
use std::{collections::{HashMap,HashSet},rc::Rc};

pub(crate) struct Source {pub document:Rc<Session>,pub origin:Rc<crate::origin::Origin>,pub tree:Option<proto::TreeNode>,pub world:HashMap<String,Xform>,pub sheets:Vec<Rc<crate::sheet_source::Prepared>>}
pub(crate) fn prepare(bytes:&[u8],place:&Xform,point_px:f32)->Result<Source,String> {
    if bytes.len()>crate::archive::LIMIT {return Err("Session exceeds the 512 MiB file limit".into());}
    let message=proto::Session::decode(bytes).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    crate::source_validate::session(&message)?;
    supported(&message)?;
    if !crate::placement::valid(&place.m)||!point_px.is_finite() {return Err("Invalid document placement or point size".into());}
    let tree=message.tree.as_ref().and_then(|tree|tree.root.clone());
    let mut guids=HashSet::new();let objects=message.objects.as_ref().ok_or("Session has no objects")?;
    macro_rules! identity {($field:ident)=>{for object in &objects.$field {if object.guid.is_empty()||!guids.insert(object.guid.as_str()) {return Err("Source GUIDs must be distinct within a document".into());}}};}
    identity!(planes);identity!(bboxes);identity!(meshes);identity!(points);identity!(lines);identity!(polylines);identity!(nurbscurves);identity!(nurbssurfaces);identity!(breps);identity!(pointclouds);identity!(instances);identity!(sheets);
    if let Some(root)=tree.as_ref() {crate::hierarchy::validate(root,&guids).map_err(str::to_owned)?;}
    let mut placed=HashSet::new();for entry in &message.xforms {if !placed.insert(entry.guid.as_str())||!crate::placement::valid(&entry.xform.as_ref().ok_or("Missing placement matrix")?.matrix) {return Err("Invalid source placement".into());}}
    let sheets=objects.sheets.iter().cloned().map(|sheet|crate::sheet_source::Prepared::new(Rc::new(sheet)).map(Rc::new).map_err(str::to_owned)).collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
    let origin=Rc::new(crate::origin::Origin::new(&message,bytes));
    let document=Rc::new(Session::from_proto(message).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?);
    let world=crate::nested_placement::prepare(&document,place).map_err(str::to_owned)?;
    Ok(Source {document,origin,tree,world,sheets})
}

fn supported(source:&proto::Session)->Result<(),String> {
    crate::instance_plan::validate(source)?;
    if !source.interactions.is_empty() {return Err("Interactions arrive with element features".into());}
    let objects=source.objects.as_ref().ok_or("Session has no objects")?;
    if !objects.elements.is_empty()||!objects.components.is_empty() {return Err("This subject cannot open assemblies yet".into());}
    Ok(())
}
```

```rust
    pub fn set_view(&mut self, view: View) {
        use std::f64::consts::{FRAC_PI_2, FRAC_PI_6, PI};
        let z = Vector::z_axis();
        let x = Vector::x_axis();
        self.orientation = match view {
            View::Front => Quaternion::from_axis_angle(z, 0.0),
            View::Back => Quaternion::from_axis_angle(z, PI),
            View::Right => Quaternion::from_axis_angle(z, FRAC_PI_2),
            View::Left => Quaternion::from_axis_angle(z, -FRAC_PI_2),
            View::Top => Quaternion::from_axis_angle(x, -FRAC_PI_2),
            View::Bottom => Quaternion::from_axis_angle(x, FRAC_PI_2),
            View::Iso => {
                let yaw_q = Quaternion::from_axis_angle(z, -FRAC_PI_6);
                let rv = yaw_q.rotate_vector(x);
                (Quaternion::from_axis_angle(rv, -FRAC_PI_6) * yaw_q).normalized()
            }
            View::IsoBack => {
                // the isometric from the opposite corner: turned half round
                let yaw_q = Quaternion::from_axis_angle(z, PI - FRAC_PI_6);
                let rv = yaw_q.rotate_vector(x);
                (Quaternion::from_axis_angle(rv, -FRAC_PI_6) * yaw_q).normalized()
            }
        };

        self.projection = Projection::Orthographic;
    }
```

```rust
    }

    pub fn reach(self, target: [f64; 3]) -> Result<f64, &'static str> {
        if target.iter().any(|v| !v.is_finite()) { return Err("Grid target must be finite"); }
        let reach = self.points()?.iter().map(|p| (p[0] - target[0]).hypot(p[1] - target[1]).hypot(p[2] - target[2])).fold(0.0, f64::max);
        if !reach.is_finite() { return Err("Grid target exceeds the depth range"); } Ok(reach)
    }
```

```rust
impl TreePanel {
    pub fn refresh(&mut self,scene:&crate::scene::Scene,selected:Vec<ObjectId>) {
        self.graph=crate::graph_edit::rows(scene);
        for doc in &scene.forest.documents{for(index,node)in doc.nodes.iter().enumerate(){if scene.groups.contains(&(doc.key,node.source.guid.clone()))&&doc.nodes[index+1..node.end].iter().filter_map(|n|n.object).any(|id|selected.contains(&id)){let mut parent=Some(index);while let Some(i)=parent{self.expanded.insert(format!("{}/{}",doc.key,doc.nodes[i].source.guid));parent=doc.nodes[i].parent;}}}}
        self.rows=crate::hierarchy::rows(scene);self.selected=selected;self.states=self.rows.iter().map(|row|(row.key.clone(),RowState {hidden:scene.view.lamps.contains(&row.key)||!row.ids.is_empty()&&row.ids.iter().all(|id|scene.view.hidden.contains(id)),locked:!row.ids.is_empty()&&row.ids.iter().all(|id|scene.view.locked.contains(id)),selectable:row.ids.iter().any(|id|scene.pick_id(id.value()).is_some()),current:crate::layer_edit::current_key(scene).as_ref()==Some(&row.key),has_faces:row.ids.iter().any(|id|scene.objects().iter().any(|mesh|mesh.id==*id)),colours:[row.ids.iter().find_map(|id|scene.view.faces.get(id).copied()),row.ids.iter().find_map(|id|scene.view.edges.get(id).copied())],group:crate::layer_edit::locate(scene,&row.key).is_ok(),root:row.depth==0,holds_current:crate::layer_edit::holds_current(scene,&row.key)})).collect();
    }
    pub fn rename_result(&mut self,error:Option<&str>) {if let Some(error)=error {self.rename_error=error.into();self.rename_focus=true;} else {self.rename=None;self.rename_error.clear();}}
```

Retain the identical current document source.

**Locate this existing block:**

```rust
pub fn open(scene:&mut Scene,bytes:&[u8])->Result<(),String> {
    let bundle=crate::archive::Bundle::decode(bytes)?;
    if !bundle.metadata.groups.is_empty() {return Err("Object groups arrive in a later subject".into());}
    let mut candidate=scene.clone();candidate.clear();let mut keys=Vec::new();
    for (source,meta) in bundle.documents.iter().zip(&bundle.metadata.documents) {keys.push(import(&mut candidate,source,&meta.name,&Xform::from_matrix(meta.place),meta.point_px)?);}
```

```rust
// A draft owns unfinished points and a cursor, so preview motion cannot become saved geometry or an Undo transaction.
pub struct Draft { pub tool: Box<dyn Tool>, pub points: Vec<[f64; 3]>, pub cursor: Option<[f64; 3]>, pub snapped: Option<crate::snap::Kind>, pub frame: crate::cplane::CPlane, pub plane_points: Option<Vec<[f64;3]>>, pub primitive:Option<crate::primitive_draft::Draft>, pub transform:Option<crate::transform_draft::Draft>,pub gather:Option<crate::gather_draft::Draft>,pub project:Option<crate::project_draft::Draft>,pub extend:Option<crate::extend_draft::Draft> }

impl Draft {
    pub fn new(shape: Shape) -> Self { Self { project:None,extend:None,gather:None,tool: Box::new(shape), points: Vec::new(), cursor: None, snapped: None, frame: crate::cplane::CPlane::Xy, plane_points: None, primitive:if let Shape::Primitive(shape)=shape {Some(crate::primitive_draft::Draft::new(shape,crate::cplane::CPlane::Xy,0).expect("Registered primitive option"))}else{None},transform:if let Shape::Transform(kind)=shape {Some(crate::transform_draft::Draft::new(kind,crate::cplane::CPlane::Xy))}else{None} } }
    pub fn prompt(&self) -> String {
        if self.plane_points.is_none(){if let Some(p)=&self.project{return p.prompt()}if let Some(e)=&self.extend{return e.prompt()}}
        if self.plane_points.is_none(){if let Some(gather)=&self.gather{return gather.prompt()}}
```

```rust
pub enum Shape { Measure, Project,Extend, Gather(&'static crate::gather_input::Recipe), Point, Line, Move, Polyline, Curve, Rectangle, Polygon {sides:usize}, Primitive(&'static crate::primitive_shape::Shape), Transform(crate::transform_draft::Kind) }

impl Tool for Shape {
    fn name(&self) -> &'static str { match self { Self::Measure=>"Measure Distance",Self::Project=>"Project To Plane",Self::Extend=>"Extend",Self::Gather(recipe)=>recipe.name, Self::Point => "Point", Self::Line => "Line", Self::Move => "Move", Self::Polyline => "Polyline",Self::Curve=>"Curve", Self::Rectangle=>"Polyline Rectangle",Self::Polygon {..}=>"Polyline Polygon",Self::Primitive(shape)=>shape.name,Self::Transform(kind)=>kind.name() } }
    fn count(&self) -> usize { match self { Self::Project|Self::Extend=>0,Self::Gather(_)=>0, Self::Point => 1, Self::Line | Self::Move | Self::Measure => 2, Self::Polyline|Self::Curve => 0,Self::Rectangle|Self::Polygon {..}=>2,Self::Primitive(_)|Self::Transform(_)=>0 } }
    fn sides(&self)->Option<usize> {if let Self::Polygon {sides}=self {Some(*sides)}else{None}}
    fn set_sides(&mut self,value:usize)->Result<(),&'static str> {
        if !(3..MAX_POINTS).contains(&value) {return Err("Polygon sides must be between 3 and 4095");}
```

```rust
    pub fn guide_overlay(&self)->crate::guide_gpu::Overlay{
        if let Some(trim)=&self.trim{return trim.overlay()}
        let mut overlay=crate::guide_gpu::Overlay::default();let red=[210./255.,40./255.,40./255.,1.];
        let Some(extend)=self.draft.as_ref().and_then(|d|d.extend.as_ref())else{return overlay};
        let label=if let Some(hover)=&extend.hover{
            overlay.wire(&hover.tail,red,true);hover.tail.last().map(|point|{let added=hover.tail.windows(2).map(|p|(p[1][0]-p[0][0]).hypot(p[1][1]-p[0][1]).hypot(p[1][2]-p[0][2])).sum::<f64>();(*point,format!("+{added:.3}"))})
        }else if !extend.error.is_empty(){extend.last.and_then(|(id,end)|{
            let owner=&extend.candidates.iter().find(|c|c.0.id==id)?.0;let (tip,_)=crate::extend_reach::tip(&owner.source,end)?;let p=tip.transformed(&owner.model);Some(([p[0],p[1],p[2]],extend.error.clone()))
        })}else{None};
        if let Some((point,word))=label{overlay.marks.push(crate::marker::Marker{center:point.map(|v|v as f32),diameter:6.,colour:red});overlay.label=Some((point,word));}overlay
    }
```

```rust
    pub fn whole_selection(&self) -> Option<ObjectId> { self.selected.filter(|_| self.selected_part.is_none()) }

    pub fn tool_line(&self, text: &str) -> Option<Result<Action, &'static str>> {
        if self.mark.is_some()&&text.trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case("Escape"){return Some(Ok(Action::CancelTool))}
```

```rust
    pub fn trimming_picks(&self)->bool{self.trim.as_ref().is_some_and(|t|t.picks())}
    pub fn trimming_parts(&self)->bool{self.trim.as_ref().is_some_and(|t|!t.picks())}
    fn commit_trim(&mut self)->Result<Change,&'static str>{
        let trim=self.trim.take().ok_or("Start Trim first")?;let before=trim.before.clone();if !trim.changed(){self.select_objects(before,false);self.tool_status="Trim cancelled · nothing changed".into();return Ok(Change::Preview)}
        let edits=trim.prepared_edits(&self.scene).map_err(|message|{self.select_objects(before.clone(),false);self.primitive_failure(message)})?;
        let result=self.history.try_edit(&mut self.scene,|scene|{for(id,pieces)in edits{crate::split_draft::adopt(scene,id,pieces)?}Ok(())});self.select_objects([],false);self.snap_index=None;result?;self.tool_status="Trimmed originals · one Undo restores them".into();Ok(Change::Scene)
    }
    fn begin_text(&mut self,words:String)->Result<Change,&'static str>{let text=crate::text_draft::Draft::new(words,self.camera.distance,self.last_text_height)?;self.apply(Action::BeginTool(crate::tool::Shape::Text))?;self.draft.as_mut().unwrap().text=Some(text);self.tool_status=self.draft.as_ref().unwrap().prompt();Ok(Change::View)}
```

```rust
    pub fn select_objects(&mut self, rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = ObjectId>, additive: bool) {
```

```rust
        let rows=crate::graph_edit::expand(&self.scene,rows);
```

```rust
        self.canvas_drag.as_ref().and_then(|d|d.preview.as_ref()).map_or_else(||self.gizmo_edit.as_ref().map_or_else(||self.transform_preview.as_ref().map_or_else(||self.edit_preview.as_ref().map_or(&self.scene,|p| &p.scene),|p|p.scene()),|g|g.preview.scene()),|p|&p.scene)
    }

    pub fn gizmo_origin(&self)->Option<[f64;3]> {
        if self.split.is_some()||self.draft.is_some()||self.canvas_drag.is_some() {return None;}
        if self.scene.clouds().iter().any(|r|r.controls_visible)||self.scene.streams().iter().any(|r|r.controls_visible) {return None;}
        if let Some(edit)=self.gizmo_edit.as_ref() {return Some(edit.origin());}
```

```rust
impl Editor {
    pub fn display_scene(&self) -> &Scene {
        if let Some(scene)=self.trim.as_ref().and_then(|t|t.preview.as_ref()){return scene}
        self.canvas_drag.as_ref().and_then(|d|d.preview.as_ref()).map_or_else(||self.gizmo_edit.as_ref().map_or_else(||self.transform_preview.as_ref().map_or_else(||self.edit_preview.as_ref().map_or(&self.scene,|p| &p.scene),|p|p.scene()),|g|g.preview.scene()),|p|&p.scene)
    }

    pub fn gizmo_origin(&self)->Option<[f64;3]> {
        if self.trim.is_some()||self.split.is_some()||self.draft.is_some()||self.canvas_drag.is_some() {return None;}
        if self.scene.clouds().iter().any(|r|r.controls_visible)||self.scene.streams().iter().any(|r|r.controls_visible) {return None;}
        if let Some(edit)=self.gizmo_edit.as_ref() {return Some(edit.origin());}
        if let Some(target)=self.selection().filter(|_|self.selected_part.is_some()) {return crate::part_preview::origin(&self.scene,target);}
```

```rust
    pub fn apply(&mut self, action: Action) -> Result<Change, &'static str> {
        if !matches!(&action,Action::Zoom(_)|Action::ZoomAt{..}|Action::PanScreen{..}|Action::Pan(_,_)|Action::Orbit(_,_)|Action::Fit|Action::FitSelected|Action::Projection(_)|Action::ResetView|Action::Isometric|Action::Background|Action::NormalView(_)|Action::Grid(_)){self.mark=None;}
        match action {
            Action::StreamRefresh=>{self.snap_index=None;return Ok(Change::Scene);},
            Action::Touch(actions)=> {let mut change=Change::View;for action in actions {let next=self.apply(action)?;if next!=Change::View {change=next;}}return Ok(change);},
            Action::CanvasStart {from,to,size,modified,pointer}=>{self.canvas_drag=crate::canvas_drag::Drag::new(from,to,size,modified);if let Some(drag)=self.canvas_drag.as_mut() {drag.pointer=pointer;}return Ok(Change::View);},
            Action::CanvasMove {screen,size}=>{if let Some(drag)=self.canvas_drag.as_mut() {if drag.released.is_none()&&drag.size==size&&screen.iter().all(|v|v.is_finite()) {drag.to=screen;drag.update(&self.camera)?;}if drag.preview.is_some() {return Ok(Change::Preview);}}return Ok(Change::View);},
```

```rust
    fn apply_action(&mut self, action: Action) -> Result<Change, &'static str> {
```

```rust
    pub fn capture(action:&Action,editor:&Editor)->Option<Self>{let request=Request::capture(action)?;let ids=request.ids(editor);let owners=ids.iter().filter_map(|id|Some((*id,editor.scene.row_guid(*id)?.to_owned(),editor.scene.placement(*id)?.m))).collect();Some(Self{request,ids,part:editor.selected_part,epoch:editor.interaction_epoch,owners})}
    pub fn valid(&self,editor:&Editor)->bool{self.epoch==editor.interaction_epoch&&self.owners.iter().all(|(id,guid,model)|editor.scene.pick_id(id.value())==Some(*id)&&editor.scene.row_guid(*id)==Some(guid)&&editor.scene.placement(*id).is_some_and(|p|p.m==*model))}


}
```

```rust
use crate::{editor::{Action, Editor}, rehydrate::ReloadKey, scene::ObjectId};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Intent {
    Command(crate::command_request::Pending),
    Move { id: ObjectId, offset: [f64; 3] },
    Delete { id: ObjectId },
    Save,
}

impl Intent {
    pub fn command(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self { Self::Command(_)=>"Command",Self::Move { .. } => "Move", Self::Delete { .. } => "Delete", Self::Save => "Save" }
    }

    pub fn capture_for(action:&Action,editor:&Editor)->Result<Option<Self>,&'static str>{
        if let Action::Translate(offset)=action{if offset.iter().any(|v|!v.is_finite()){return Err("Move needs finite coordinates")}if offset.iter().all(|v|*v==0.){return Ok(None)}}
        if let Some(pending)=crate::command_request::Pending::capture(action,editor){if !pending.keys(editor).is_empty(){return Ok(Some(Self::Command(pending)))}}
        if editor.selected_objects().len()>1{return Ok(None)}Self::capture(action,editor.whole_selection())
    }

    pub fn keys(&self, editor: &Editor) -> Result<Vec<ReloadKey>, &'static str> {
        match self {
            Self::Command(pending)=>{if !pending.valid(editor){return Err("Command target changed before loading sources")}Ok(pending.keys(editor))},
            Self::Save => Ok(editor.reload_keys()),
            Self::Move { id, .. } | Self::Delete { id } => {
                let Some(row) = editor.scene.objects().iter().find(|row| row.id == *id) else {
```

```rust
fn command_alias(line:&str)->String{
    let words=line.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>();let compact=words.join("").to_ascii_lowercase();
    for(alias,current)in[("object","Select Object"),("face","Select Face"),("edge","Select Edge"),("escape","Escape"),("esc","Escape")]{if compact==alias{return current.into()}}
    for count in (1..=words.len().min(2)).rev(){if ["ConstructionPlane","CPlane"].iter().any(|name|words[..count].join("").eq_ignore_ascii_case(name)){return std::iter::once("Construction Plane").chain(words[count..].iter().copied()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ")}}
    line.into()
}
```

```rust
pub fn parse(line: &str) -> Result<Command, String> {
    let line=command_alias(line);let line=line.as_str();
    let line=crate::gather_registry::alias(line);
    let alias=crate::transform_commands::alias(&line);let line=alias.as_str();
    let words: Vec<_> = line.split_whitespace().collect();
    let (name, count) = matched(&words).ok_or("Unknown command. Type Help.")?;
    if name=="Measure Distance"{if words.len()>count+2{return Err("Measure Distance takes two points".into())}return Ok(Command::Editor(Action::MeasureDistance(words[count..].join(" "))))}
    if name=="Split"{return if words.len()==count{Ok(Command::Editor(Action::Split))}else{Err("Split takes no arguments".into())};}
    if let Some(action)=crate::editing_commands::action(name,&words[count..]){return action.map(Command::Editor);}
```

```rust
}

pub fn parse(line: &str) -> Result<Command, String> {
    if line.len()>65536{return Err("command exceeds 64 KiB".into())}
    let line=command_alias(line);let line=line.as_str();
    let line=crate::gather_registry::alias(line);
    let alias=crate::transform_commands::alias(&line);let line=alias.as_str();
    let words: Vec<_> = line.split_whitespace().collect();
    let (name, count) = matched(&words).ok_or("Unknown command. Type Help.")?;
    if name=="Add Group"{let name=words[count..].join(" ");if name.contains('/'){return Err("A group name must be without /".into())}return Ok(Command::Editor(Action::AddGroup(name)))}
    if name=="Trim"{return match &words[count..]{[]=>Ok(Command::Editor(Action::Trim)),[a,b]=>{let(a,b)=(a.parse::<f64>().map_err(|_|"Trim takes two domain parameters")?,b.parse::<f64>().map_err(|_|"Trim takes two domain parameters")?);Ok(Command::Editor(Action::Interval(crate::interval_geometry::Interval::Trim(a,b))))},_=>Err("Trim takes two domain parameters".into())}}
    if name=="Text"{return Ok(Command::Editor(Action::TextBegin(words[count..].join(" "))))}
    if name=="Select By Name"{return crate::selection_query::Query::name(&words[count..]).map(|query|Command::Editor(Action::Query(query)))}
    if name=="Select Small"{if words.len()!=count+1{return Err("Select Small takes one positive length".into())}let value=words[count].parse::<f64>().ok().filter(|v|v.is_finite()&&*v>0.).ok_or("Select Small wants a length above zero")?;return Ok(Command::Editor(Action::Query(crate::selection_query::Query::Small(value))))}
    if name=="Arrowhead"{let head=match &words[count..]{[v]if v.eq_ignore_ascii_case("None")=>session_rust::Arrowhead::NONE,[v]if v.eq_ignore_ascii_case("Start")=>session_rust::Arrowhead::START,[v]if v.eq_ignore_ascii_case("End")=>session_rust::Arrowhead::END,[v]if v.eq_ignore_ascii_case("Both")=>session_rust::Arrowhead::BOTH,_=>return Err("Arrowhead None Start End Both".into())};return Ok(Command::Editor(Action::Arrowhead(head)))}
    if name=="Measure Distance"{if words.len()>count+2{return Err("Measure Distance takes two points".into())}return Ok(Command::Editor(Action::MeasureDistance(words[count..].join(" "))))}
    if name=="Split"{return if words.len()==count{Ok(Command::Editor(Action::Split))}else{Err("Split takes no arguments".into())};}
    if let Some(action)=crate::editing_commands::action(name,&words[count..]){return action.map(Command::Editor);}
```

```sh
npm --prefix ../session_tests run course -- dependencies 16.5
cd workspace/journey
REGEN_PROTO=0 cargo build --lib --locked --target wasm32-unknown-unknown -j1
REGEN_PROTO=0 CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=1 trunk serve --port 8780

npm --prefix ../session_tests run course -- save 16.5
```
